## 🟡 2026-10-01 09:30 — KPM: PUSHED `79b3e7d..acdb0cf`; NEW: vault skipped after sign-in (diagnosed, no code)
His *"i sign in my google account and its skipped everything now, no master vault UI just straight inside the app"*. Not today's push (canvas-only diff). Cause found by reading: the side-menu logout (BiohazardTheme.jsx:192 "Terminate Session?") calls `signOut(auth)` + reload but NOT `clearGrace()` (only App.jsx:3069 does, used on the lockout screens). So the 5-minute grace record survives logout, and the same account signing back in within 5 min of his last tap gets the vault back (App.jsx:2954). vaultGrace.js says logout must end it. Fix = one line, `clearGrace()` in that handler + a guard.
**WAITING ON ALDI:** ❓ *"Before you signed in, did you log out with the button in the side menu (it asks "Terminate Session?"), and had you used the app less than 5 minutes before that?"* · 🔴 fix it (one line) - yes/no.
